## Reproduce

**Shell:** powershell

### Check out the evaluator

```powershell
git clone 'https://github.com/markcowl/azure-rest-api-specs.git' evaluator
git -C evaluator checkout 'f37db08ada6edd33d546129d99410fcce79aeb5a'
corepack pnpm --dir evaluator install --frozen-lockfile
```

### Prepare the immutable analyzer

```powershell
$prepared = node evaluator/eng/tools/breaking-change-evaluator/cmd/breaking-change-evaluator.js prepare --typespec-revision 65f3ad98e7d82a2fdb26e7c24a4587a4db9cd52a | ConvertFrom-Json
if ($prepared.artifactDigest -ne '95835342e0e018bc513e3f639a2b36ec641918b5db3084bdb380fce3bd8f6cc5') { throw 'Prepared analyzer digest does not match the report' }
```

### Check out the evaluated target

```powershell
git clone --filter=blob:none --no-checkout 'https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs.git' target
git -C target fetch --no-tags origin 67d29f055c6c0ca0ff8262181837b1e9fc049c4a a1388911c672997637ae5f1689556ce3ea551335
git -C target checkout --detach a1388911c672997637ae5f1689556ce3ea551335
corepack pnpm --dir target install --frozen-lockfile
```

### Rerun the complete evaluator

```powershell
node evaluator/eng/tools/breaking-change-evaluator/cmd/breaking-change-evaluator.js evaluate --pr 'https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/45742' --tool-revision 65f3ad98e7d82a2fdb26e7c24a4587a4db9cd52a --json-output evaluator.json --markdown-output evaluator.md
```

### Run the TypeSpec analyzer for specification/mission/resource-manager/Microsoft.Mission/Mission

```powershell
Push-Location target
node $prepared.executable 'specification/mission/resource-manager/Microsoft.Mission/Mission' --base-ref 67d29f055c6c0ca0ff8262181837b1e9fc049c4a --phase same-version --json-output '../specification-mission-resource-manager-Microsoft.Mission-Mission-phase-a.json' --markdown-output '../specification-mission-resource-manager-Microsoft.Mission-Mission-phase-a.md' --fail-on-breaking
node $prepared.executable 'specification/mission/resource-manager/Microsoft.Mission/Mission' --base-ref 67d29f055c6c0ca0ff8262181837b1e9fc049c4a --json-output '../specification-mission-resource-manager-Microsoft.Mission-Mission-phase-b-evidence.json' --markdown-output '../specification-mission-resource-manager-Microsoft.Mission-Mission-phase-b-evidence.md' --fail-on-breaking
```
